# PLACECLI local process-boundary receipt

This receipt records observed synthetic producer results for v13 PLACECLI (IF-0-PLACECLI-1 and
IF-0-PLACECLI-2). It grants no live activation, toolkit cutover, E2B activation or public
conformance:

- live routes stay disabled;
- no production OpenShell backend is registered;
- `policies.json` stays `pending_qualification`.

Every coordinator, bridge peer and credential proxy below is an offline fake.

## How the producers ran

The producer groups V0–V3 ran on `ai`, serially, from the pushed tree in a private scratch
worktree:

- the interpreter was the tree's locked uv venv (`uv sync --locked`, with `uv.lock`
  unchanged), and an isolated `-I` import pre-check passed;
- the project admission lock was held for the whole run;
- PSI gates were checked before each group (memory full avg10 ≤ 1, I/O full avg10 < 5);
- each group ran under `timeout 900`, with no xdist and no cache provider.

V4 then built an installed wheel the way `scripts/gate_a_cleanroom.sh` does and ran V0 and V3
against it, from a standalone copy of the tests. Repo-only tests skip there by design.

Counts are reduced from copied JUnit per-node outcomes, never from collection counts. Group
totals cover every file in the group, including the landed suites that SL-1 extends. Per-node
outcomes cover the four producer files this phase created.

## Mutation controls

Mutation controls substitute one production guard at a time and show the producer's check
going red. Each one is paired with an intact witness.

- **Lifecycle:** the provider allowlist, caps readiness, headroom, undeclared egress, OD-7,
  separate inventories, the timeout label, the inclusive settle bound, and reaching
  commit/execute from reserve.
- **Bridge:** modes, socket reuse, service exposure and teardown.
- **CLI:** a JSON refusal on stdout for schema-invalid input.

## What the receipt pins

Following agent-harness#1955, the receipt pins no shared or frequently changed file:

- no file in the ownership map's shared table;
- no `docs/*.md` file;
- no workflow;
- no inventory file.

It pins only the sources, tests and fixtures this phase created, plus the frozen PLACECLI
artifacts. `test_seat_reference_inventory.py` enforces launch-reference classification on
every PR.

## Still pending

- live activation and the toolkit's three switch conditions (v13 ACTIVATE);
- the launch service that consumes the reservation, the bridge and `config_sha256`
  (v13 LANEEXEC);
- E2B, which waits on the owner's account and spend activation.
